## Owner's latest broken-game report
Treat collision/solidness, GUI/menu/control accessibility, ground/road surfaces, car assembly/wheels, colour/PBR, shadows and real asset integration as release-blocking defects. New candidates are staged only: none may be counted implemented in complete cars/tracks until actually wired and tested. Added fixes pending cloud verification include shared structural collision roles (buildings/props/vehicles, not material-name-only walls), excluding ground/decals from phantom walls, giving AI the same static contacts as the player, visible desktop mouse/touch controls with pause/pit/camera actions and focus/restart reset, a menu-first launcher, preserving ground geometry normals, and reduced shadow normal bias. Inspect the new cloud screenshots before accepting these changes. No game window was launched locally for these fixes.

## Expanded rebuild authority
The owner explicitly authorizes rebuilding the entire game, re-engineering every track, and replacing the physics engine if the evidence requires it. Existing architecture is not a constraint. Preserve original assets and a working revision for comparison; all substantial computation remains on GitHub. A rewrite is not evidence of improvement: require equivalent or better race rules, valid wheel/body geometry, stable fixed-step dynamics, real static/vehicle contacts, continuous collision handling, grounded wheels/suspension, usable GUI/input and inspected build evidence. The current planar simulation plus height projection must not be represented as fully three-dimensional rigid-body physics. Decide from measured defects and replacement tests; avoid random parameter changes or wholesale activation of unreviewed meshes.

## Dark-road investigation reached an actual source defect
Surface comparison40927606656 PASSED all12 capture processes; reviewed track05 authored/default with shadows on/off at full resolution. Road remains black in both shadow settings and both texture modes. Process times66?141seconds on software llvmpipe: old preview120second budget is insufficient for some cases, but preserve those timeout failures and do not call software timings hardware performance acceptance.
Found WindingFixer used cross/normal dot<0 as a reason to flip. Godot explicitly uses CLOCKWISE front faces (official ArrayMesh docs https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/classes/class_arraymesh.html); the outward normal is opposite that cross product. Existing code inverted healthy imported faces (old logs1387/1388flipped), explaining a strong shading/culling defect. Corrected sign, added healthy PlaneMesh/reversed-plane/idempotence regression. Need cloud checks and actual rendered comparison; do not yet claim final road fix.

## Dark-road investigation reached an actual source defect
Surface comparison40927606656 PASSED all12 capture processes; reviewed track05 authored/default with shadows on/off at full resolution. Road remains black in both shadow settings and both texture modes. Godot explicitly uses CLOCKWISE front faces (official ArrayMesh docs https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/classes/class_arraymesh.html); the outward normal is opposite that cross product.
